help mad
 <strong>mad</strong> - Mean or median absolute deviation
    This MATLAB function returns the mean absolute deviation of the values
    in X.

    Syntax
      y = <strong>mad</strong>(X)
      y = <strong>mad</strong>(X,flag)
      y = <strong>mad</strong>(X,flag,'all')
      y = <strong>mad</strong>(X,flag,dim)
      y = <strong>mad</strong>(X,flag,vecdim)

    Input Arguments
      <a href="matlab:web /Applications/MATLAB_R2025b.app/help/stats/mad.html#mw_003a3b84-a391-4ac3-8012-354f00116911">X</a> - Input data
        vector | matrix | multidimensional array
      <a href="matlab:web /Applications/MATLAB_R2025b.app/help/stats/mad.html#mw_40ea4498-70a3-4a55-bd7d-08a1697a66a8">flag</a> - Indicator for the type of deviation
        0 (default) | 1
      <a href="matlab:web /Applications/MATLAB_R2025b.app/help/stats/mad.html#mw_89036ff0-cc5e-414c-848a-212e03e5f002">dim</a> - Dimension
        positive integer
      <a href="matlab:web /Applications/MATLAB_R2025b.app/help/stats/mad.html#mw_7a2e4343-ff3f-45ef-9c10-b22e9f440414">vecdim</a> - Vector of dimensions
        positive integer vector

    Output Arguments
      <a href="matlab:web /Applications/MATLAB_R2025b.app/help/stats/mad.html#mw_4e442bcc-1c77-4ff9-94a5-15be309d8a9a">y</a> - Mean or median absolute deviation
        scalar | vector | matrix | multidimensional array

    Examples
      <a href="matlab:openExample('stats/RobustnessComparisonOfScaleEstimatesExample')">Compare Robustness of Scale Estimates</a>
      <a href="matlab:openExample('stats/MeanAndMedianAbsoluteDeviationsOfAllValuesExample')">Mean and Median Absolute Deviations of All Values</a>
      <a href="matlab:openExample('stats/FindMedianAbsoluteDeviationAlongGivenDimensionExample')">Find Median Absolute Deviation Along Given Dimension</a>
      <a href="matlab:openExample('stats/FindMeanAbsoluteDeviationAlongVectorOfDimensionsExample')">Find Mean Absolute Deviation Along Vector of Dimensions</a>

    See also <a href="matlab:help std -displayBanner">std</a>, <a href="matlab:help range -displayBanner">range</a>, <a href="matlab:help iqr -displayBanner">iqr</a>
